			NULL, "那一瞬间，我们周围的世界也慢慢变白，像融化一样模糊起来。", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

//梨花rvS14/05/220500519.「は……羽入ぅぅうぅ――ッッ！！」
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#6972c1>梨花</color>", NULL, "<color=#6972c1>梨花</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 5, "ps3/s14/05/220500519", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「は……羽入ぅぅうぅ——ッッ！！」",
			NULL, "「羽…………羽入啊啊啊——！！」", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	DisableWindow();
	DrawScene("text/hanyu_18", 1000 );
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 12, "ps3/s14/12/221100302", 256, TRUE);
	Wait( 7000 );
	DrawScene("white", 1000 );
	DrawScene("black", 1000 );

	DisableWindow();
	// (backup) SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 3000 );
	DrawBustshotWithFiltering(6, "cinema", "x", 1, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 25, 1300, TRUE );
	DrawBustshot(7, "title02", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 26, 3000, TRUE );
	Wait( 2000 );
	DrawBustshot(6, "black", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 25, 3000, TRUE );
	Wait( 1000 );
	FadeBustshotWithFiltering( 7, "x", 1, FALSE, 0, 0, 1000, TRUE );
	DrawScene( "black", 3000 );
	// (backup) SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

}
